Abstract: Simple analytic pulse-shaping techniques are of great practical utility in quantum control, with prime examples being the DRAG method for suppressing leakage in superconducting microwave gates and the transitionless-driving approach to shortcuts-to-adiabaticity. Standard versions of these methods require two orthogonal control channels, with the second channel effectively breaking time-reversal symmetry. This appears to rule out their use in settings with only a single real-valued control field, such as the kind of baseband flux control that is common in many superconducting circuit architectures. We show here that a simple analytic pulse-shaping technique derived via a Magnus expansion is effective even with just a single baseband control channel. We demonstrate its efficacy by simulating a two-qubit gate between transmons realized with a tunable coupler and baseband flux pulses. Our corrections dramatically reduce non-adiabatic leakage caused by ramping the coupler: for realistic device parameters, leakage in a fast iSWAP gate is suppressed by up to three orders of magnitude. Our approach is general, goes beyond simply suppressing unwanted spectral weight at leakage transitions, and can be applied to a variety of platforms.

</details>


### [87] [All causally separable quantum processes are quantum circuits with classical control of causal order](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20774)
*Julian Wechs,Alastair A. Abbott,Cyril Branciard*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明了先前工作给出的多体因果可分离性的充分条件也是必要条件，从而给出多体因果可分离性的完整刻画。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 因果（非）可分离性描述局部量子操作之间因果序是否有定义或不确定。多体情形下的因果（非）可分离性此前已被引入和研究，但其中一个充分条件是否也是必要条件尚属开放问题。

Method: 采用一种新颖的“相干隐形传态技术”（coherent teleportation technique）。

Result: 证明Wechs、Abbott和Branciard（New J. Phys. 21, 013027 (2019)）中识别的因果可分离性充分条件同时也是必要条件，从而完成对多体因果可分离性的完整刻画。

Conclusion: 所有因果可分离过程均可实现为广义量子电路，其中操作之间的顺序由经典方式控制，即“具有因果序经典控制的量子电路”。

Abstract: The concept of causal (non)separability describes whether the causal order between parties that perform local quantum operations is well-defined or indefinite. Causal (non)separability in the general multipartite setting was introduced and studied in [Oreshkov and Giarmatzi, New J. Phys. 18, 093020 (2016); Wechs, Abbott, and Branciard, New J. Phys. 21, 013027 (2019)]. We resolve an open problem from these earlier works by showing -- using a novel "coherent teleportation technique" -- that a sufficient condition for causal separability identified in [Wechs, Abbott, and Branciard, New J. Phys. 21, 013027 (2019)] is also necessary, and thus provides a complete characterisation of multipartite causal separability. A consequence of this result is that all causally separable processes admit a realisation as generalised quantum circuits in which the order between the operations is classically controlled, known as "quantum circuits with classical control of causal order".

</details>


### [88] [Asymptotically Good Quantum Locally Testable Codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20780)
*William Gay,Fernando Granha Jeronimo*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文显式构造了量子比特上渐近优的量子局部可测试码（qLTC），即具常码率、常相对距离与常权重局部测试器且测试可靠性为常数的显式量子LDPC CSS码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子局部可测试码（qLTC）是量子PCP猜想与量子纠错的核心对象，但长期缺乏同时满足常码率、常相对距离与常数可靠性局部测试器的显式构造。此前多数qLTC构造或依赖非显式方法，或无法同时兼得上述三个渐近优参数，因此需要给出可显式描述的优质量子LDPC CSS码。

Method: 通过构造量子LDPC CSS码来实现：在量子比特上设计具有常码率与常相对距离的CSS码族，并为其引入常权重的局部测试器，同时保证测试器的可靠性（soundness）为常数。构造为显式（explicit）的，即码的定义与测试器可被有效描述。

Result: 得到显式qLTC族，同时具备常数码率、常数相对距离、常数权重局部测试器以及常数可靠性，从而在量子比特上实现渐近优的量子局部可测试码。

Conclusion: 该工作给出了渐近优qLTC的显式量子LDPC CSS码构造，为量子PCP猜想及量子纠错理论提供了重要进展，说明常参数条件下的qLTC可被显式实现。

Abstract: We construct explicit families of asymptotically good quantum locally testable codes over qubits. More precseily, we construct explicit quantum LDPC CSS codes over qubits having constant rate, constant relative distance and constant weight local testers with constant soundness.

</details>


### [89] [Parallel quantum channel discrimination and numerical ranges in tensor product subspaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20781)
*Adam Bílek,Paulina Lewandowska,Ryszard Kukulski*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究并行方案下的完美量子信道分辨问题，利用数值范围（numerical range）的基本性质，提出结合二分法的SDP方法，可在关于拷贝数线性的时间内计算完美分辨所需的量子态，并给出实现完美分辨的最少信道拷贝数；据此正面解决了Duan、Guo、Li和Li提出的Conjecture 1。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子信道分辨在量子信息论中至关重要，而"完美"分辨的情形尤具理论价值。现有工作尚缺乏在并行方案下高效计算完美分辨量子态的方法，也未能确定实现完美分辨所需的最少信道拷贝数，且Duan等人提出的关于并行使用次数的Conjecture 1尚未得到解决，因此有必要建立系统性的分析与判定工具。

Method: 聚焦并行方案下的完美量子信道分辨任务：构建半定规划（SDP）表述，并配合二分（bisection）过程，使得完美分辨所需量子态可在关于拷贝数线性的时间内求解；同时导出实现完美分辨的最少信道拷贝数。全部结果均建立在数值范围的概念及其基本性质之上，核心是证明了矩阵子空间张量积的数值范围的最小角等于各子空间数值范围最小角之和。

Result: 所提SDP加二分法能以关于拷贝数线性的时间计算完美分辨的量子态，并确定实现完美分辨所需的最少信道拷贝数；进而正面证实了Duan、Guo、Li和Li（arXiv:1605.02294, IEEE ISIT 2016）的Conjecture 1，刻画了完美分辨某类算子子空间所需的并行使用次数。

Conclusion: 数值范围为并行完美量子信道分辨提供了有效工具，其关于张量积的最小角可加性这一关键性质是全部结论的基础；该框架既给出高效算法与最少拷贝数刻画，也解决了悬而未决的Conjecture 1，为后续信道分辨研究提供了新的分析范式。

Abstract: Quantum channel discrimination plays a crucial role in quantum information theory. Of particular interest is the case in which the channels can be discriminated perfectly. In this work, we focus on the perfect quantum channel discrimination task in a parallel scheme. We develop an SDP formulation combined with a bisection procedure to compute a quantum state for perfect discrimination in time linear in the number of copies. In addition, we obtain the minimal number of copies of quantum channels to achieve perfect discrimination. Thanks to that, we settle in the affirmative Conjecture 1 of Duan, Guo, Li and Li [arXiv:1605.02294, IEEE ISIT 2016], which characterizes the number of parallel uses needed to discriminate perfectly a distinguished family of operator subspaces. All our results are obtained using the notion and basic properties of the numerical range. In particular, the key fact that we prove and use is that the minimal angle of the numerical range of a tensor product of matrix subspaces equals the sum of the minimal angles of the numerical ranges of the individual subspaces.

</details>


### [90] [Locally optimized variational evolution for quantum many-body systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20802)
*Carolin Wille,Max Marvell,Lauren Stewart,Max Murphy,Vinul Wimalaweera,Lesley Gover,Andrew G. Green*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于局部约化密度矩阵代价函数的变分时间演化原理，利用热化系统中局部可观测量复杂度不随系统规模增长的特点，在保留短时相干动力学的同时简化后期演化，并给出基于矩阵乘积态的闭式运动方程及量子-经典混合实现方案，已在Quantinuum H2与IBM Heron处理器上验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统量子优势依赖全局波函数的指数级复杂度，但局部可观测量的模拟成本并不随之增长，而受与系统尺寸无关的"内禀局域复杂度"支配。这一区别在热化系统中尤为关键：局部可观测量会丢失微观细节、弛豫至仅由少数参数决定的平衡值。因此，直接以全局态保真度为目标会浪费这一简化资源。

Method: 提出变分时间演化原理：用定义在局部约化密度矩阵上的代价函数替代全局态保真度。具体算法基于矩阵乘积态的局域优化，并推导出类似于时间依赖变分原理（TDVP）的闭式运动方程。同时给出量子-经典对应版本：用量子设备评估局部代价，配合对散粒噪声和硬件噪声鲁棒的优化策略。

Result: 该演化在短时保持相干的动力学行为，在后期利用热化带来的简化。在Quantinuum H2和IBM Heron处理器上的原理验证实验成功复现了特征局域动力学。

Conclusion: 以局部约化密度矩阵为代价函数的变分原理，为多体动力学模拟提供了一条规避全局指数复杂度的路径，兼具闭式方程的经典实现与鲁棒的量子-经典混合实现，并已在现有量子硬件上得到初步验证。

Abstract: Conventional quantum advantage in many-body dynamics is based on avoiding the simulation cost on a classical computer that arises from the extensive exponential complexity of the global wavefunction. Local observables, however, do not inherit this extensive complexity and may instead be governed by an intrinsic local complexity that is independent of the total system size. This distinction is particularly relevant in thermalising systems, where local observables lose memory of microscopic details and relax towards equilibrium values determined by only a few parameters. Here we introduce a variational time-evolution principle that exploits this distinction by replacing global-state fidelity with a cost function defined on local reduced density matrices. The resulting evolution retains coherent short-time dynamics while exploiting the simplification produced by thermalisation at later times. The concrete algorithm we propose is based on locally optimising matrix-product states and admits closed-form equations of motion analogous to the time-dependent variational principle. We show that the same variational principle has a quantum-classical counterpart, combining quantum evaluation of the local cost with an optimisation strategy robust to both shot and hardware noise. Proof-of-principle implementations on Quantinuum H2 and IBM Heron processors recover the characteristic local dynamics.

</details>


### [91] [Environment Alignment and Redundant Record Formation in Imperfect-CNOT Quantum Darwinism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20823)
*Aleksander Lasek,Paweł Horodecki*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究不完美CNOT模型（随机高斯耦合、纯非相互作用环境量子比特）中，制备、相互作用角度与场无序如何共同影响量子达尔文主义的冗余记录形成，揭示非单调响应源于条件分支分离的几何结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子达尔文主义通过环境冗余记录解释客观信息的涌现。已有工作表明不完美记录可被放大，环境自演化也可增强或抑制记录形成，但制备、相互作用角度与场无序三者的联合作用机制尚不清楚，需要系统研究其如何共同决定记录能力。

Method: 构建含随机高斯耦合的不完美CNOT模型，环境为纯态、非相互作用量子比特。引入单一对准参数Λ刻画无场情形下条件态可区分性对制备与相互作用角度的依赖；对比均匀局域场与等均方根强度的高斯分布场；用条件分支分离的几何解释非单调响应；给出片段信息的精确表达式并进行最多24个环境量子比特的数值模拟，分析有限观测窗口内的记录可靠性。

Result: 无场时，单一对准参数Λ决定条件态可区分性的制备与角度依赖。增大相互作用不完美度或局域场强度既可改善也可抑制记录，呈非单调响应，其根源在于条件分支分离的几何。对纯初始环境，Z基对Holevo信息始终最优，故场辅助记录无需改变所记录的系统可观测量。等均方根强度下，高斯分布场相对均匀场会拓宽有益与有害场效应的范围。精确片段信息表达式与最多24量子比特的数值模拟量化了高平均信息与跨片段、有限观测窗口内可靠记录之间的区别。

Conclusion: 研究将局域信息获取的几何结构与稳健记录所需的片段尺寸联系起来。分支可区分性分析仅依赖各环境量子比特的条件演化，可推广至其他保持系统指针基的相互作用。

Abstract: Quantum Darwinism explains objective information through redundant environmental records. Earlier work established that imperfect records can be amplified and that environment self-evolution can enhance or suppress their formation. We investigate how preparation, interaction angle, and field disorder combine in an imperfect-CNOT model with random Gaussian couplings and pure, noninteracting environment qubits. We find that, without fields, a single alignment parameter $Λ$ determines the preparation and interaction-angle dependence of conditional-state distinguishability. Increasing interaction imperfection or local field strength can improve or suppress recording. We explain this nonmonotonic response through the geometry of conditional branch separation. For the pure initial environments considered here, the $Z$ basis remains optimal for Holevo information, so field-assisted recording requires no change of the recorded system observable. Comparing uniform local field strengths with Gaussian-distributed strengths at equal root-mean-square strength shows how disorder broadens both beneficial and detrimental field effects. Exact expressions for fragment information and numerical simulations with up to 24 environment qubits quantify the distinction between high mean information and reliable records across fragments and throughout a finite observation window. These results connect the geometry of local information acquisition to the fragment sizes needed for robust recording. The branch-distinguishability analysis itself requires only the conditional evolution of each environment qubit and extends to other interactions that preserve a system pointer basis.

</details>

### [92] [A State-Space Model of Figured-Bass Realization: Local Constraints, Coupled Voices, and Polynomial-Time Solvability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19397)
*Evan Unit Lim*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: 本文将通奏低音实现形式化为带硬约束与可选偏好代价的四声部实现问题，用四边形顶点表示声部、有序声部状态表示和弦，将合法进行建模为分层图中的路径，并证明在固定声部数、有限音域与固定局部规则下，可行性与最小代价实现均为多项式时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通奏低音实现本质上是在单个和弦内部与相邻和弦之间做出一系列受约束的选择，但既有讨论多为经验性规则，缺少显式的数学刻画。作者希望为一种受限的、考试风格的四声部实现问题建立精确的形式模型，以明确哪些要求是硬约束、哪些只是可选的偏好代价，并厘清该模型的可计算性边界。

Method: 将音高拼写、音域、和弦成员资格、重复、省略、间距、交错、重叠、旋律进行、连续纯音程及若干解决要求逐一表达为谓词；区分硬约束与可选偏好代价。把四个带标记的音符可视化为一四边形顶点，计算上表示为一个有序声部状态；合法进行构成分层图中的路径，从而把实现问题转化为图上的可行性与最短路/最小代价问题。通过二拍、四拍、八拍的实例演示合法性、优化以及贪心选择的失效。

Result: 证明在声部数固定、音域与音符字母表有限、局部规则固定的条件下，可行性判定与最小代价实现都是多项式时间可解的；当音域、音符字母表与相邻事件规则固定时，图操作数目与事件数成线性关系。实例分析展示了一个贪心选择会导致失败的案例。

Conclusion: 该结果为所陈述的形式模型给出了明确的复杂度结论，说明受限四声部通奏低音实现的可行性与最优求解可在多项式时间内完成。作者强调结论仅限于该形式模型，并不主张所有音乐判断都能由局部谓词完全刻画。

Abstract: Figured-bass realization can be described as a sequence of choices constrained both within each sonority and between successive sonorities. This paper gives an explicit mathematical model of a restricted, examination-style four-part realization problem. Pitch spelling, range, chord membership, doubling, omission, spacing, crossing, overlap, melodic motion, consecutive perfect intervals, and selected resolution requirements are expressed as predicates. We distinguish hard constraints from optional preference costs. Four labeled notes are represented visually as the vertices of a quadrilateral and computationally as one ordered voicing state. Legal progressions become paths through a layered graph. We prove that feasibility and minimum-cost realization are polynomial-time problems for a fixed number of voices with explicit finite note domains and fixed local rules. For fixed ranges, a fixed note alphabet, and adjacent-event rules, the number of graph operations is linear in the number of events. Worked two-, four-, and eight-beat examples illustrate legality, optimization, and the failure of a greedy choice. The result concerns the stated formal model; it is not a claim that every musical judgment is captured by local predicates.

</details>


### [93] [A Cross-Lingual Acoustic Disease-Alignment Framework for Respiratory Health Assessment from Spontaneous Speech](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19398)
*Roksana Khanom,Raghib Asfak Tasnim,Bodrun Nahar Bithi,Shafia Shirin Supty,Saiful Islam Raju,Ashok Agrawala,Nirupam Roy*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: 提出CL-DAF跨语言疾病对齐框架，从自发语音中筛选跨语言一致的疾病相关声学特征，提升多语言呼吸健康评估的泛化性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自发语音可作为可扩展、非侵入的呼吸健康评估信号，但疾病相关声学变化与语言特异的语音变异相混淆，导致可解释且跨语言泛化的模型难以构建。

Method: 收集201名英语与75名孟加拉语说话人数据，构建272维共同声学表示；使用带符号秩双列效应量与语言不变性评分量化疾病对齐程度；据此提出CL-DAF框架，筛选跨语言疾病效应一致的声学维度。

Result: 孟加拉语自发语音可区分COPD与对照（AUC 0.85）；但133个特征在跨语言间疾病方向反转，完整表示跨语言迁移差（孟加拉语→英语AUC 0.49）。CL-DAF筛选出26个疾病对齐特征，使英语→孟加拉语与孟加拉语→英语的AUC分别提升至0.825和0.722。

Conclusion: 跨语言对齐可分离病理相关声学变化与语言依赖变异，为强调病理而非语言差异的多语言临床语音模型奠定基础。

Abstract: Spontaneous speech offers a scalable, noninvasive signal for respiratory health assessment, yet interpretable models that generalize across languages remain challenging because disease-related acoustic changes are confounded by language-specific phonetic variation. We present CL-DAF, a Cross-Lingual Disease-Alignment Framework that identifies acoustic dimensions whose disease effects remain consistent across languages. Using 201 English and 75 newly collected Bangla speakers, we construct a common 272-dimensional acoustic representation and quantify disease alignment using signed rank-biserial effects and the Language Invariance Score. We first show that spontaneous Bangla speech separates COPD from controls (AUC 0.85); however, 133 features reverse their disease direction across languages and the full representation transfers poorly (AUC 0.49 from Bangla to English). CL-DAF isolates 26 disease-aligned features that raise AUCs to 0.825 and 0.722 from English to Bangla and Bangla to English, respectively. These findings provide a foundation for multilingual clinical speech models emphasizing pathology over language-dependent variation.

</details>


### [94] [CoRELoop: Parameter-Efficient Controlled Recurrent Refinement for Audio Deepfake Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19818)
*Kunyu Feng,Yuxiang Wang,Li Wang,Wan Lin,Zhizheng Wu*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: 提出CoReLoop，在冻结的SSL音频深伪检测器上训练轻量循环细化模块与低秩适配器，实现无额外数据的迭代式泛化增强，两遍推理将14个跨域测试集的EER从4.85%降至3.74%（约10M/598M可训参数），并可用halting head自适应选择迭代深度达到3.73%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 音频深伪检测器的跨域泛化能力不足，难以应对未见攻击类型，而收集覆盖所有潜在攻击的训练数据不切实际。因此希望在已有预训练SSL检测器基础上、不增加数据也不改变其原始参数的前提下提升泛化性能。但直接复用编码器输出作为下一轮输入反而会降低检测效果，说明简单的循环复用并不可行。

Method: 提出CoReLoop循环细化框架，核心包括三点：使循环输入适配冻结编码器、控制状态更新、将细化输出与冻结分类器对齐。仅训练轻量细化模块和循环专属的低秩适配器（LoRA类），使用原始训练数据，不改变原检测器参数，同时保留其首遍预测结果。此外设计可选的halting head，对每条语音自适应决定细化深度。

Result: 在14个跨域测试集上，24层模型经两遍（two passes）细化将pooled EER由4.85%降至3.74%；可训练参数约10M，而模型总参数为598M。加入选择性halting head后，平均仅需1.18遍即可取得3.73%的pooled EER。

Conclusion: 通过冻结原检测器、仅训练轻量循环细化模块与低秩适配器，CoReLoop能够在不增加数据、不改变原始参数的情况下，将循环复用有效转化为可用的细化机制，显著提升跨域泛化，并以极小参数代价实现近似"即插即用"的性能增益；halting head进一步在精度与计算开销之间实现灵活权衡。

Abstract: Generalizing to unseen attacks remains challenging for audio deepfake detectors, and collecting training data covering all potential attacks is impractical. We explore recurrent refinement in an already-trained SSL-based detector without additional data or changes to its original parameters. However, directly recycling encoder outputs as inputs degrades detection in our diagnostic. We propose CoReLoop, which makes this reuse effective by adapting recurrent inputs to the frozen encoder, controlling state updates, and aligning refined outputs with the frozen classifier. By training only lightweight refinement modules and loop-specific low-rank adapters on the original data, CoReLoop enables additional refinement while preserving the detector's original first-pass prediction. On 14 cross-domain test sets, the 24-layer model reduces pooled equal error rate (EER) from 4.85% to 3.74% with two passes, with approximately 10M trainable parameters out of 598M. To selectively apply this refinement, an optional halting head chooses the depth for each utterance, achieving 3.73% pooled EER with an average of 1.18 passes.

</details>


### [95] [Robust Workflow Generation via Adversarial Learning for Audio Deepfake Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20063)
*Xiang Li,Pin-Yu Chen,Wenqi Wei*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: 提出ROGUE框架，通过双智能体对抗学习动态编排多个检测工具，构建鲁棒的音频深伪检测工作流，在真实扰动和分布偏移下显著提升鲁棒性与泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语音合成与语音转换技术快速发展使音频深伪日益逼真，带来严重安全风险。现有检测方法在受控条件下性能优异，但在真实世界的扰动与损坏下泛化能力不足，难以满足实际部署需求。

Method: 将检测工作流生成建模为序贯决策问题，引入双智能体范式：扰动智能体生成音频扰动，策略智能体在扰动条件下学习选择与执行检测工具；通过对抗学习实现扰动感知的工具选择、自适应执行策略，并提升对分布偏移的鲁棒性。

Result: 在多个数据集和真实世界损坏条件下开展大量实验，ROGUE在鲁棒性和泛化性上均持续优于强基线方法。

Conclusion: 对抗优化的检测工作流生成方法能有效构建面向真实部署的可靠音频深伪检测系统，验证了多工具动态编排范式的有效性。

Abstract: The rapid advancement of speech synthesis and voice conversion technologies has made audio deepfakes increasingly realistic, posing serious security risks in practical applications. While existing detection methods achieve strong performance under controlled conditions, they often fail to generalize under real-world perturbations and corruptions. In this paper, we propose ROGUE, a framework that dynamically constructs robust detection workflows by orchestrating multiple detection tools. ROGUE formulates workflow generation as a sequential decision-making problem and introduces a dual-agent paradigm, where a perturbation agent generates audio perturbations and a policy agent learns to select and execute detection tools under perturbed conditions. Through adversarial learning, ROGUE enables perturbation-aware tool selection, adaptive execution strategies, and improved robustness to distribution shifts. Extensive experiments across multiple datasets and real-world corruptions demonstrate that ROGUE consistently outperforms strong baselines in both robustness and generalization. Our results highlight the effectiveness of adversarially optimized workflow generation for building reliable audio deepfake detection systems in real-world deployment settings.

</details>


### [96] [CircleMatch: Prototype Matching with Circular Temporal Statistics for Tiny Keyword Spotting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20070)
*Jiajun Sun,Zhe Gao*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: 提出CircleMatch匹配框架，用极少参数实现关键词识别，通过频带独立压缩、原型匹配与无参数循环聚合实现分类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语音设备上的关键词识别需在严格参数预算下保持高准确率，且需适应不同词表规模，现有方法难以兼顾。

Method: 编码器独立压缩各频带并融合为帧特征；帧特征与可学习的类别原型匹配生成时间响应曲线；无参数循环聚合将时间编码为角度，总结响应分布与相对时序进行分类。开发Circle-D4/D8/D16/D32四个微型变体，12类设置下约1k至7k参数。

Result: 在Speech Commands v1/v2及Multilingual Spoken Words Corpus的英语、西班牙语Micro子集上，多次随机种子实验显示微小模型具备竞争性准确率。定性分析表明原型响应具有近似平移等变性并适应时间压缩。

Conclusion: CircleMatch以极少参数实现有竞争力的KWS性能，验证了原型匹配与循环聚合的有效性，代码与权重已开源。

Abstract: Keyword spotting (KWS), the task of identifying predefined words in speech, is a core capability of voice-enabled devices. Achieving high KWS accuracy under tight parameter budgets across different vocabulary sizes remains challenging. We present CircleMatch, a matching framework enabling KWS with very few parameters. Its encoder independently compresses frequency bands and fuses them into frame features. These features are then matched against learned class-specific prototypes to produce temporal response curves. Parameter-free circular aggregation encodes time as angles and summarizes response distributions and relative timing for classification. We develop four tiny variants, Circle-D4, Circle-D8, Circle-D16, and Circle-D32, ranging from approximately 1k to 7k parameters in the 12-class setting. Experiments with multiple random seeds on Speech Commands v1/v2 and the English and Spanish Micro subsets of the Multilingual Spoken Words Corpus demonstrate competitive accuracy with tiny models. Our qualitative analysis further suggests approximate shift equivariance of prototype responses and adaptation to temporal compression. Code and model weights are available at https://github.com/ora942878/CircleMatch.

</details>


### [97] [Multi-Dimensional Prosody Judgment For Live Streaming Speech Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20124)
*Zifan Guan,Longyu Lu,Junan Zhang,Zhizheng Wu,Meiguang Jin,Junfeng Ma*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: 提出LPJ与D-LPJ两个评测器，将Gemini能力蒸馏到Qwen3-Omni，解决多维评测中的"判定耦合"问题，实现低成本、解耦的细粒度韵律评测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 评估直播流式语音合成需要捕捉情感、语调、能量等细粒度、高度表现力的韵律特征，传统MOS预测器无法胜任；而Gemini等专有LLM虽可胜任，但大规模推理与强化学习反馈成本过高。

Method: 1) 提出Live-ProsodyJudge（LPJ）：从Gemini蒸馏到Qwen3-Omni的低成本成对评测器；2) 指出标准多维评测的"判定耦合"缺陷：评判器倾向于将所有单维度分数与其整体偏好对齐，把多维评分体系坍缩为单一偏好位；3) 提出D-LPJ：去除整体判定目标以防盲目跟随，在SFT中掩蔽不确定的成对维度，并引入新的span-local GRPO策略，将归一化优势严格应用于对应的理由片段。

Result: 在精编人工标注测试集上，10样本平衡顺序的LPJ点准确率高于单次Gemini调用；D-LPJ成功产生独立、解耦的维度判定。在Best-of-8 TTS候选选择锦标赛中，高置信度情况下LPJ所选话语有85.29%落在人类前三名。

Conclusion: LPJ实现低成本且优于单次Gemini调用的韵律评测，D-LPJ有效解决判定耦合、产出解耦的维度判断，验证了其用于细粒度TTS偏好优化的有效性。

Abstract: Evaluating live streaming speech synthesis (TTS) requires assessing fine-grained, highly expressive prosody such as emotion, intonation, and energy which traditional MOS predictors fail to capture. While proprietary Large Language Models (LLMs) like Gemini can evaluate these aspects, they are too costly for massive inference and reinforcement learning feedback. To address this, we first introduce Live-ProsodyJudge (LPJ), a cost-effective pairwise evaluator distilled from Gemini into Qwen3-Omni. However, we identify a critical flaw in standard multi-dimensional evaluation: verdict coupling. The judge tends to lazily align all individual dimension scores with its overall preference, collapsing a rich multi-dimensional rubric into a single preference bit. To resolve this, we further propose Decoupled-Live-ProsodyJudge (D-LPJ). D-LPJ eliminates the overall verdict target to prevent blind following, masks uncertain pair-dimensions during Supervised Fine-Tuning(SFT), and introduces a novel span-local GRPO strategy that applies normalized advantages strictly to their corresponding rationale spans. Evaluated on highly curated human-annotated test sets, 10 sample balanced-order LPJ achieves higher point accuracy than a single Gemini call, while D-LPJ successfully produces independent,decoupled dimension judgments. Furthermore, in a Best-of-8 TTS candidate selection tournament, the LPJ-selected utterance falls within the human top-3 in 85.29% of high-confidence cases, demonstrating its efficacy for fine-grained TTS preference optimization.

</details>


### [98] [Music Hallucination in Audio-Language Models: A Hierarchical Formulation and Empirical Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20195)
*Yu Liu,Jiahui Liu,Zhilin Liu,Cong Cao,Fangfang Yuan,Yuling Yang,Pin Xu,Yanbing Liu*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: 本文首次针对音频-语言模型中的音乐幻觉开展分层、多范式的实证研究，提出五层感知接地失败框架与MuseDiag诊断框架，评测九个模型并检验两种免训练缓解方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 音频-语言模型日益倾向于生成自信但缺乏音频依据的音乐描述，即音乐幻觉问题，但此前缺乏针对音乐领域、分层且跨范式的系统性实证研究。

Method: 将音乐幻觉形式化为跨五层（声音事件、时间属性、音调属性、风格、情感）的层级式感知接地失败；提出多范式诊断框架MuseDiag，采用基于矛盾的验证；评测四个开源与五个闭源共九个模型；并提出两种免训练缓解方法ADD-M（音乐音频依赖感知解码）与TPA（分类体系引导的感知锚定）。

Result: （1）人声误感知为九个模型共有的普遍弱点，音调感知是架构区分的主要轴，Audio-Flamingo-3稳定领先，其后模型排序出现明显重排，揭示范式特异的脆弱性画像；（2）肯定偏置、生成模式效应与层级特异的感知局限均与观察模式存在实证关联，多分析证据收敛但非严格因果归因；（3）ADD-M与TPA可在探针任务中降低幻觉，但增益因模型而异，且常无法迁移至自由生成。

Conclusion: 音乐幻觉缓解必须跨范式进行评估；单一探针任务的改善不足以证明缓解方法的有效性。

Abstract: Audio-language models increasingly generate confident music descriptions that are unsupported by the input audio. We present, to our knowledge, the first music-specific, layer-wise, multi-paradigm empirical study of hallucination in audio-language models and formulate it as a hierarchical perceptual grounding failure across five layers: sound events, temporal properties, tonal attributes, style, and emotion. We introduce MuseDiag, a multi-paradigm diagnostic framework with contradiction-based verification, and evaluate nine models (four open-source and five closed-source). We find that (1) vocal misperception is a universal weakness across all nine models, tonal perception is a major axis of architectural differentiation, and Audio-Flamingo-3 remains the stable leader while substantial reordering below it reveals paradigm-specific vulnerability profiles; (2) affirmative bias, generation-mode effects, and layer-specific perceptual limitations are each empirically associated with the observed patterns, with convergent evidence from multiple analyses rather than strict causal attribution; and (3) our two training-free mitigation methods, Audio-Dependency-Aware Decoding for Music (ADD-M) and Taxonomy-Guided Perceptual Anchoring (TPA), can reduce hallucination in probing, but their gains vary by model and often do not carry over to free-form generation, showing that music hallucination mitigation must be evaluated across paradigms.

</details>


<div id='nlin.PS'></div>

# nlin.PS [[Back]](#toc)

### [99] [The Origin of Imperfection Sensitivity in the Buckling of Cylindrical Shells](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19436)
*Tian Yang,Tobias M. Schneider*

Main category: nlin.PS

TL;DR: 薄壁圆柱壳轴压屈曲的缺陷敏感性源于完美壳中已有的局域平衡态：缺陷通过破坏平移对称性“钉扎”这些平衡态，改变其与基态的连接方式，从而使触发屈曲的分岔类型发生切换，导致屈曲阈值非单调、不连续变化及模态突变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 薄壁圆柱壳轴压屈曲是典型的亚临界失稳，对微小几何缺陷极其敏感，微小几何偏差即可引起屈曲阈值大幅变化。作者旨在揭示这种敏感性的物理与数学来源。

Method: 采用数值延拓与分岔分析，系统改变单个局域高斯缺陷的深度与尺寸，追踪缺陷壳的分岔结构与平衡态连接关系。

Result: 缺陷壳的失稳起源于完美壳中已存在的局域平衡态；缺陷破坏平移对称性后将这些平衡态钉扎，并改变其与不完美基态的连接方式。缺陷几何的微小变化可切换触发屈曲的分岔类型，造成屈曲阈值的非单调与不连续变化，以及屈曲模态的突变。

Conclusion: 缺陷敏感性不仅是“对缺陷幅值的敏感”，更是“底层分岔结构对缺陷几何的敏感”。

Abstract: Buckling of thin cylindrical shells under axial compression, a classical example of a subcritical instability, is highly sensitive to small imperfections, with minute geometric variations causing large changes in buckling threshold. To uncover the origin of this sensitivity, we use numerical continuation and bifurcation analysis while systematically varying the depth and size of a single localized Gaussian defect. We show that the instabilities of the imperfect shell originate from localized equilibria already present in the perfect shell. By breaking translation symmetry, the defect pins these equilibria and changes how they connect to the imperfect base state. Small changes in defect geometry can thereby switch the bifurcation that triggers buckling, producing non-monotonic and discontinuous changes in buckling threshold and abrupt changes in buckling mode. Imperfection sensitivity is therefore not simply sensitivity to imperfection magnitude, but sensitivity of the underlying bifurcation structure to imperfection geometry.

</details>


<div id='eess.AS'></div>

# eess.AS [[Back]](#toc)

### [100] [Decaf: A privacy preserving speech codec using speaker disentanglement and canonical voice conversion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19304)
*Md Shakhrul Iman Siam,Dushyant Sharma,Stanislav Yu. Kruchinin,Peter Skala*

Main category: eess.AS

TL;DR: DECAF是一种隐私保护神经语音编解码器，通过分离说话人信息与内容信息，在极低比特率下实现语音匿名化并保持ASR性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在语音传输中保护说话人隐私，同时不牺牲语言内容与下游ASR性能，解决语音匿名化与内容保真之间的矛盾。

Method: 发送端将语音编码为说话人无关的内容嵌入，经残差向量量化压缩后传输，不含任何说话人信息；接收端使用端点间先验共享的规范说话人嵌入进行波形重建。框架基于自监督表示上的信息瓶颈，配合独立的说话人嵌入分支实现说话人-内容解耦，并引入基于CTC的辅助目标使内容表示与ASR任务对齐。

Result: 在0.5 kbps比特率下，说话人验证系统EER最高达43.5%；相比最先进方法，词错误率相对降低33.2%，保持有竞争力的ASR性能。

Conclusion: DECAF在极低比特率下有效实现说话人语音混淆与内容保留，兼顾隐私保护与ASR可用性。

Abstract: We present DECAF, a privacy preserving neural speech codec that obfuscates a speaker's voice while preserving linguistic content while maintaining automatic speech recognition (ASR) performance at very low bitrates, inspired by decaffeination. At the transmitter end, speech is encoded into speaker independent content embeddings, which are compressed using residual vector quantization and transmitted without any speaker related information. At the receiver, a canonical speaker embedding, shared a priori between endpoints, is used for waveform reconstruction, enabling deterministic and consistent obfuscation of a speaker's voice. The proposed framework leverages an information bottleneck applied to self supervised representations, along with a separate speaker embedding branch, to achieve effective speaker content disentanglement. We further incorporate a CTC-based auxiliary objective, encouraging content representations that are well aligned with downstream ASR tasks. We show that DECAF operating at a bit rate of 0.5 kbps achieves an Equal Error Rate (EER) of up to 43.5% for a speaker verification system, while maintaining competitive ASR performance, yielding a relative reduction in word error rate of 33.2% compared to a state of the art method.

</details>


### [101] [PersianVox: A Prosody-Aware Approach for Speech Dataset Generation from In-the-Wild Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19324)
*Saeedreza Zouashkiani,Soheil Khalesi,Saman Soleimani Roudi,Sajjad Amini,Shahrokh Ghaemmaghami*

Main category: eess.AS

TL;DR: 本文提出 PersianVox，一个面向波斯语的全自动语音语料构建流水线，可从无标注网络数据生成高质量多说话人语音数据集，并发布了首个波斯语语音质量评估方法的对比基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低资源语言的零样本 TTS 受限于大规模高保真语音数据集的稀缺。传统基于对齐的方法需要罕见的逐字转写；而常规 in-the-wild 流水线依赖单模型 ASR 与基于静音的分割，易导致转写错误和韵律被截断。

Method: 提出 PersianVox 全自动流水线，整合：(1) 基于声学 turn-detection 的韵律感知分割策略，以保持语言完整性并优化长上下文建模所需的话语时长；(2) 双模型一致性机制，利用两种不同架构的模型在无真实标注条件下过滤不可靠转写。

Result: 该流水线产出 2,400 小时多说话人数据集，为迄今最大的开源波斯语语音资源；同时提供首个波斯语语音质量评估方法的对比基准，并发布人工标注子集。

Conclusion: PersianVox 为低资源语言的零样本 TTS 数据构建提供了可行方案，缓解了高质语音语料稀缺问题，并通过基准与标注子集推动后续研究。

Abstract: Advancement of zero-shot text-to-speech synthesis is currently hindered for low-resource languages by the scarcity of large-scale, high-fidelity speech datasets. Traditional alignment-based methods require rare verbatim transcripts, while standard in-the-wild pipelines often rely on single-model automatic speech recognition and silence-based segmentation, leading to transcription errors and truncated prosody. To address these challenges for the Persian language, this paper introduces PersianVox, a fully automated pipeline designed to generate high-quality speech corpora from unlabeled web data. Our approach integrates a novel prosody-aware segmentation strategy that utilizes acoustic turn-detection to preserve linguistic completeness and optimize utterance duration for long-context modeling. Furthermore, we employ a dual-model agreement mechanism, leveraging two distinct model architectures to filter unreliable transcriptions without ground truth. This pipeline yields a 2,400-hour multi-speaker dataset, the largest open-source speech resource available for Persian to date. Additionally, we provide the first comparative benchmark of speech quality assessment methods for Persian, releasing a human-annotated subset to facilitate future research.

</details>


### [102] [Inverse Problems in Musical Instrument Modeling: A Structured Taxonomy and Review](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19345)
*Xinmeng Luan,Gary Scavone*

Main category: eess.AS

TL;DR: 本文对乐器建模中的逆问题进行结构化分类与系统综述，提出一个统一框架，并将其归纳为十类任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 音乐声学中逆问题（如物理建模声音合成、乐器建模、设计与优化）广泛存在，但因其病态特性及对测量噪声的强敏感性而极具挑战，缺乏统一的分类与分析框架。

Method: 提出结构化的分类法（taxonomy），对乐器建模中的逆问题进行系统综述，建立统一的分类与分析框架，并据此进行任务划分。

Result: 将乐器建模中的逆问题划分为十类任务：力学参数估计、几何参数估计、损耗估计、边界条件估计、模态参数估计、激励与发音参数估计、声音匹配或模型参数拟合、乐器设计与优化、场重建/表征与分离、物理模型辨识与发现。

Conclusion: 该工作为乐器建模逆问题提供了统一的分类与分析框架，有助于系统化理解与后续研究。

Abstract: Inverse problems arise in a wide range of applications in musical acoustics, including physics-based sound synthesis, musical instrument modeling, design, and optimization. However, these problems are inherently challenging due to their ill-conditioned nature and strong sensitivity to measurement noise. This paper presents a structured taxonomy and systematic review of inverse problems in musical instrument modeling, providing a unified framework for their classification and analysis. We categorize inverse problems in musical instrument modeling into ten distinct tasks: mechanical parameter estimation; geometric parameter estimation; loss estimation; boundary condition estimation; modal parameter estimation; excitation and articulatory parameter estimation; sound matching or model parameter fitting; instrument design and optimization; field reconstruction, characterization and separation; physical model identification and discovery.

</details>


### [103] [Multimodal Conversational Context for LLM-Based ASR: Data Construction, Training, and Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19765)
*Longhao Li,Jian Tang,Yuxiang Kong,Jie Chen,Binbin Zhang,Lei Xie,Xiangang Li*

Main category: eess.AS

TL;DR: 本文提出一个面向LLM-based ASR的多模态对话上下文框架，包含场景可控数据管线、可扩展多模态上下文训练与系统评测，并构建MM-ContextASR Bench。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对话上下文可提供跨轮次的语义与声学线索，但依赖历史转写文本会传播识别错误，并丢失发音与说话人信息。

Method: 围绕实体及其易混淆形式构建对话，将历史用户语音与助手文本回复交错用于监督微调，实现多模态上下文训练；提出MM-ContextASR Bench，覆盖五种场景评测上下文理解与实体纠错。

Result: 在Qwen3-Omni与Step-Audio-2-mini上实验，揭示其在处理无关与错误历史方面的局限；所提数据构建与训练提升上下文利用，多模态上下文在两模型上取得最高整体实体召回；口音、方言与目标说话人ASR实验表明历史语音具有价值。

Conclusion: 多模态对话上下文优于仅文本上下文，能有效提升LLM-based ASR的上下文利用与实体纠错能力；基准数据与评测代码已公开。

Abstract: Conversational context provides semantic and acoustic cues across turns for automatic speech recognition (ASR), but relying on historical transcripts can propagate recognition errors and discard pronunciation and speaker information. We present a multimodal conversational-context framework for LLM-based ASR that integrates a scenario-controlled data pipeline, scalable multimodal context training, and systematic evaluation. We construct dialogues around entities and their confusable forms and interleave historical user speech with assistant text responses for supervised fine-tuning. We also introduce MM-ContextASR Bench, which evaluates contextual understanding and entity error correction across five scenarios. Experiments with Qwen3-Omni and Step-Audio-2-mini reveal limitations in handling irrelevant and erroneous history and show that our data construction and training improve context utilization, with multimodal context achieving the highest overall entity recall on both models. Further experiments on accent, dialect, and target-speaker ASR demonstrate the value of historical speech. The benchmark data and evaluation code are publicly available at https://github.com/llh666521/MM-ContextASR.

</details>


### [104] [Consensus-Guided Shared-Specific Tri-View Learning for Speech Emotion Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19826)
*Bing Huang,Yujian Ma,Xikun Lu,Xianquan Jiang,Jinqiu Sang*

Main category: eess.AS

TL;DR: 本文提出TriCGF（三视图共识引导融合）框架，将频谱图、MFCC和HuBERT三种表示分解为共性与视图特有分量，通过跨视图共识学习和视图门控整合进行融合，并加入软差异正则化抑制冗余。在IEMOCAP与EmoDB上取得领先性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语音情感识别（SER）受益于异构声学表示，但来自同一话语的不同视图既包含重叠的情感证据，也包含依赖具体表示的线索。直接融合可能传播冗余信息或掩盖互补细节，因此需要一种能区分并合理利用共性与特有信息的融合策略。

Method: 提出TriCGF，联合建模频谱图、Mel频率倒谱系数（MFCC）和HuBERT表示。对每个视图先分解为共性分量与视图特有条件；跨视图共识学习（Cross-view Consensus Learning）将共性分量聚合为全局参考；视图门控整合（View-wise Gated Integration）将该参考与各视图特有条件自适应结合；同时引入软差异正则化（soft difference regularizer）抑制信息过度重叠。

Result: 在说话人无关（speaker-independent）评测下，TriCGF在IEMOCAP上取得74.19%加权准确率（WA）和75.17%非加权准确率（UA），在EmoDB上取得94.36% WA和94.28% UA，在两个数据集上均优于代表性SER方法。

Conclusion: 通过显式分离共性与视图特有分量并以共识引导融合，TriCGF有效缓解了多视图直接融合的冗余与信息遮蔽问题，验证了异构声学表示协同建模在SER中的有效性。

Abstract: Speech emotion recognition (SER) benefits from heterogeneous acoustic representations, but views derived from the same utterance contain both overlapping emotional evidence and representation-dependent cues. Direct fusion may therefore propagate redundant information or obscure complementary details. To address this issue, we propose Tri-view Consensus-Guided Fusion (TriCGF) for jointly modeling spectrogram, Mel-frequency cepstral coefficients, and HuBERT representations. TriCGF organizes each view into common and view-specific components before fusion. Cross-view Consensus Learning aggregates the common components into a global reference, while View-wise Gated Integration adaptively combines this reference with each view-specific component. A soft difference regularizer further discourages excessive information overlap. Under speaker-independent evaluation, TriCGF achieves 74.19% weighted accuracy (WA) and 75.17% unweighted accuracy (UA) on IEMOCAP, and 94.36% WA and 94.28% UA on EmoDB, outperforming representative SER methods on both datasets.

</details>


### [105] [Foreground Voice Activity Detection: Learning Speaker Selectivity from Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19856)
*Guangzhao Yang,Muhammad Huzaifah,Yu Pan,Jinya Sakurai,Ningjie Bai*

Main category: eess.AS

TL;DR: 论文提出 Foreground VAD（FVAD）任务，只把以持续存在定义的“主导说话人”判为正类；关键贡献是“前景标签 + 竞争说话人混音”的自动监督方案，并给出选择性指标 BG-FAR 与评测基准。轻量流式模型 Mamba-FVAD 在选择性上超过商用 VAD 和基于注册的说话人感知系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 VAD 将所有人声（包括背景说话人）都视为有效活动，在嘈杂多人场景中会造成识别过载、打断轮次切换并触发误 barge-in；现有方案未定义或解决“仅前景主导说话人”的帧级检测问题。

Method: 形式化 FVAD：帧同步、免注册，正类仅为主导说话人，且单说话人时退化为常规 VAD。核心方法是自动生成的增广策略——仅前景标签与竞争说话人混音配对，无需人工标注；同时提出由前景 F1 门控的背景误报率 BG-FAR，并构建 Mix-Interference 基准及适配 VOiCES 的远场评测。

Result: 在等规模骨干对比中，Mamba 与 LSTM 表现相当，更长上下文的注意力模型未更优，说明监督方式比时序建模能力影响更大。Mamba-FVAD 在前景选择性上优于商用 VAD 与基于注册的说话人感知系统，常规 VAD 上保持竞争力，CPU 每帧延迟 1–2 ms。

Conclusion: 前景选择性主要由训练监督决定；所提自动增广方案可训练出轻量流式 FVAD 模型，在低延迟下兼顾前景选择性与常规 VAD 性能。

Abstract: Voice activity detection (VAD) fronts most voice-agent pipelines, yet production detectors treat all human speech, background talkers included, as valid activity; in crowded settings this floods recognition, stalls turn-taking, and triggers false barge-in. We formalize Foreground VAD (FVAD): a frame-synchronous, enrollment-free task in which only the dominant speaker, defined by sustained presence rather than instantaneous loudness, is positive, and which reduces to conventional VAD when a single speaker is present. We show that foreground selectivity is largely governed by training supervision: the crucial ingredient is an augmentation recipe pairing foreground-only labels with competing-speaker mixing, generated fully automatically without human annotation. To quantify selectivity we introduce the Background False-Alarm Rate (BG-FAR), gated by foreground F1, and build a controlled benchmark, Mix-Interference, complemented by an adapted VOiCES for real-world far-field evaluation. Across equal-size backbones, Mamba and LSTM perform on par while a longer-context attention model is no better, suggesting that training supervision plays a substantially larger role than temporal modeling capacity in achieving foreground selectivity. The resulting lightweight streaming model, Mamba-FVAD, outperforms commercial VADs and enrollment-based speaker-aware systems in foreground selectivity while staying competitive on conventional VAD, at 1-2 ms per-frame CPU latency.

</details>


### [106] [Alignment-Path Distillation from Non-streaming ASR-LLMs for Streaming Speech Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20121)
*Yan Jia,Kai Huang,Junjie Chen,Feng-Long Xie,Xu Tang,Yao Hu*

Main category: eess.AS

TL;DR: 提出对齐路径蒸馏框架，用非流式ASR-LLM的注意力对齐路径替代强制对齐，构建流式交错训练序列，并结合logit与隐状态蒸馏。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流式交错ASR-LLM依赖独立声学模型（如CTC）产生的强制对齐，该对齐与LLM-based ASR自身学到的对齐不一致，限制流式识别性能。

Method: 从非流式教师模型的软文本-音频注意力中提取单调对齐路径，用其构建交错训练序列；同时引入logit蒸馏与隐状态蒸馏，学习教师输出分布与内部表示。

Result: 仅用教师对齐路径（无logit/隐状态蒸馏）相对强制对齐降低5.2%相对错误率；两者均用蒸馏时降低3.9%，平均发射延迟相近但闪烁更高；完整框架相比强制对齐且无蒸馏降低16.6%相对错误率。

Conclusion: 教师模型衍生的对齐路径优于外部强制对齐，与logit、隐状态蒸馏结合可显著提升流式ASR-LLM性能，代价是闪烁增加。

Abstract: In this paper, we propose an alignment-path distillation framework for streaming automatic speech recognition (ASR) with large language models (LLMs). Interleaved streaming ASR-LLMs use forced alignments (FA) from alignment models, such as those trained with connectionist temporal classification (CTC), to construct speech-text training sequences. However, alignments obtained from a separate acoustic model may be inconsistent with those learned by LLM-based ASR. This motivates us to transfer alignment information from a non-streaming ASR-LLM to improve streaming recognition. Specifically, we extract monotonic alignment paths from a non-streaming teacher's soft text-audio attention and use them to construct interleaved training sequences. The framework also includes logit and hidden-state distillation to learn from the teacher's output distributions and internal representations. Experimental results show that, without logit or hidden-state distillation, training with teacher-derived alignment paths achieves a 5.2% relative error rate reduction compared with training using forced alignments. When both models use logit and hidden-state distillation, teacher-derived alignments yield a 3.9% relative error rate reduction, with similar mean emission latency but higher flicker. The complete framework achieves a 16.6% relative error rate reduction compared with training using forced alignments without logit or hidden-state distillation.

</details>


### [107] [State-Space-Based FIR Filtering on a Quantum Computer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20331)
*Roope Salmi,Davide Rocchesso,Vesa Välimäki*

Main category: eess.AS

TL;DR: 本文提出了有限脉冲响应（FIR）滤波器的量子实现方案，通过状态空间框架用酉量子线路执行基于采样的信号处理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多信号处理任务需要大量计算。量子计算有望加速特定任务，但算法必须围绕量子力学的限制进行设计。FIR滤波器是经典信号处理中广泛使用的工具，因此需要一种与其适配的量子算法实现。

Method: 引入状态空间框架，用酉量子线路执行基于采样的信号处理；提出量子延迟门作为离散时间信号处理中延迟线的量子对应；针对酉线路天然描述无损系统的局限，通过模拟投影算子来实现低通及其他有界滤波器。滤波器可并行化并作为更大量子算法的组成部分。

Result: 通过量子线路模拟器对FIR滤波器实现进行了测试与演示，并指出结合量子振幅估计和未来容错量子硬件具有潜在加速空间。

Conclusion: 该工作为FIR滤波器提供了可并行、可组合的量子实现框架，为量子信号处理的实际应用奠定了基础。

Abstract: Many signal processing tasks require intensive computations. Quantum computing promises to accelerate certain tasks, but algorithms must be designed around the limitations of quantum mechanics. This paper provides a quantum implementation of finite impulse response (FIR) filters, which are a widely used tool in classical signal processing. The filter can be parallelized and composed as part of larger quantum algorithms, with potential for speedups using quantum amplitude estimation and future fault-tolerant quantum hardware. To accomplish these properties, we introduce a state-space framework wherein sample-based signal processing is performed with unitary quantum circuits. We present the quantum delay gate as the quantum analog of the delay line in discrete-time signal processing. Unitary circuits intrinsically describe lossless systems, but we also implement lowpass and other bounded filters by emulating a projection operator. The FIR filter implementation is tested and demonstrated with a quantum circuit simulator.

</details>


### [108] [Beyond the Stability--Plasticity Frontier in Streaming Target Speaker Extraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20463)
*Yuesheng Ma,Linyang He,Nima Mesgarani*

Main category: eess.AS

TL;DR: 流式目标说话人提取需在目标静音、被干扰掩蔽或声学漂移时维持目标状态。现有方法用人工规则更新存储的嵌入。作者在22种配置下证明这类方法处于稳定性-可塑性前沿：即使拥有完美目标活动信息，也无法兼顾鲁棒性与自适应。为此提出通过闭环流式回路元训练说话人状态动态，得到41k参数锚定快速权重（AFW）记忆，在严重失配下比最佳启发式提升3.0 dB，静音30 s后仍保持在静态注册0.9 dB以内，运行时开销低于5%。GRU对照实验证实增益非AFW独有，但AFW更小且可解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流式目标说话人提取需持续维护"提取谁"的状态，但目标可能静音、被干扰掩蔽或声学漂移偏离注册语音。现有系统将该状态表示为存储嵌入，并由人工设计的规则更新（如置信度门控、理想活动门控），难以同时兼顾对目标缺失的鲁棒性与对注册-混合失配的自适应。

Method: 在22种配置上（含置信度门控与oracle活动门控更新）刻画启发式方法的稳定性-可塑性前沿；提出通过闭环流式回路元训练说话人状态动态，使更新器暴露于自身受污染的证据；设计41k参数的锚定快速权重（AFW）记忆；并用门控循环单元（GRU）控制实验验证增益来源。

Result: 在严重失配下，AFW比最佳启发式提升3.0 dB；目标静音30 s后仍与静态注册相差在0.9 dB以内；运行时开销低于5%。GRU对照表明增益并非AFW特有，而AFW更小、更可解释：严重失配下其写残差增大并与目标而非干扰对齐。

Conclusion: 人工规则的说话人状态更新受限于稳定性-可塑性前沿，即使有完美目标活动信息也无法兼顾鲁棒性与自适应；通过闭环元学习说话人状态动态可突破该前沿，AFW以极小参数量与低开销实现更优性能且具可解释性，代码已开源。

Abstract: Streaming target speaker extraction must maintain a representation of whom to extract while the target may fall silent, be masked by interference, or drift acoustically away from enrollment. Existing systems typically hold this state as a stored embedding updated by hand-designed rules. Across 22 configurations, including confidence-gated and oracle-activity-gated updates, we show that this family lies on a stability-plasticity frontier: even perfect target-activity information cannot combine robustness to target absence with adaptation to enrollment-mixture mismatch. We therefore meta-train speaker-state dynamics through the closed streaming loop, exposing the updater to its own contaminated evidence. Our proposed 41k-parameter anchored fast-weights (AFW) memory moves beyond the measured heuristic frontier, gaining 3.0 dB over the best heuristic under severe mismatch while staying within 0.9 dB of static enrollment after 30 s of absence, at under 5% runtime overhead. A gated recurrent unit (GRU) control confirms that the gain is not AFW-specific, while AFW is smaller and more interpretable: under severe mismatch, its write residual grows and aligns with the target rather than the interferer. Code is publicly available at https://github.com/ym2976/anchor-fast-weight.

</details>


### [109] [Model-Agnostic and Language-Agnostic Voice Pipeline Improvement for the Agriculture Domain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20504)
*Aakash Singh,Lakshmi Pedapudi,Chandrashekar M S,Sanyam Singh,Naga Ganesh,Vineet Singh*

Main category: eess.AS

TL;DR: 面向小农户的语音问答场景，提出模块化、模型无关的ASR质量提升流程，在多说话人录音上效果尤为显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FarmerChat 面向小农户提供多语言（文本/语音/图片）农业咨询，语音是重要入口；但田间录音含机械噪声、背景媒体、多说话人干扰，且农业专有词汇（作物、病虫害、化学品、数量词）密集，通用ASR表现差，而这些词恰是查询语义核心。

Method: 在不微调、不替换底层ASR模型的前提下，构建模块化流程：门控音频增强 → 说话人日志与目标说话人选择 → ASR → 基于加权农业词典的领域感知纠错 → 检测不可靠转写的质量门控。仅说话人日志模块经微调，其余均使用现成模型并通过统一接口集成，故为模型无关。

Result: 在印地语、泰卢固语、奥里亚语的人工标注录音上，以WER和领域加权错误率评估。全语料WER相对降低：云ASR模型16–23%，端侧模型5%；多说话人录音上云模型降低32–42%，端侧模型16%；所有降幅均具统计显著性。最大提升来自多说话人场景，目标说话人选择可阻止竞争语音进入转写。

Conclusion: 针对性预处理、说话人选择与领域感知后处理可在保留底层ASR模型的情况下显著提升农业语音转写质量；模块化设计便于复用与替换，尤其适用于噪声与重叠语音的田间咨询场景。

Abstract: FarmerChat is Digital Green's AI-powered agricultural advisory assistant for smallholder farmers, who access it in their own language through text, voice, or photographs. Voice is a critical channel for this population, yet field-recorded speech is challenging for general-purpose automatic speech recognition (ASR) because recordings frequently contain machinery noise, background media, competing speakers, and domain-specific agricultural vocabulary. These conditions disproportionately affect crop, pest, chemical, and quantity terms that carry the meaning of a farmer's query.
  We present a modular, model-agnostic pipeline for improving ASR quality in FarmerChat without fine-tuning or replacing the underlying ASR model. The pipeline combines gated audio enhancement, speaker diarization and target-speaker selection, ASR, domain-aware correction using a weighted agricultural lexicon, and a quality gate for detecting unreliable transcripts. Only the diarization stage is fine-tuned; all other stages use off-the-shelf models behind common interfaces.
  We evaluate the pipeline on human-annotated FarmerChat recordings in Hindi, Telugu, and Odia using word error rate (WER) and a domain-weighted error rate that gives greater importance to agricultural terminology. The largest improvements occur on multi-speaker recordings, where target-speaker selection prevents competing speech from entering the transcript. Across the full corpus, the pipeline reduces WER by 16-23% relative on three cloud ASR models and by 5% on an on-device model. On multi-speaker recordings, the reductions are 32-42% for the cloud models and 16% for the on-device model. All reported reductions are statistically significant. These results show that targeted preprocessing, speaker selection, and domain-aware post-processing can substantially improve agricultural speech transcription while preserving the underlying ASR model.

</details>


### [110] [A Deep Neural Network for Predicting Continuous Human EEG Across the Auditory Pathway in Response to Sound](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20595)
*Thomas J Stoll,Ross K Maddox*

Main category: eess.AS

TL;DR: 该研究构建了一个以双耳声波形为输入、直接预测高采样率EEG的因果神经网络基础模型，并在ABR、TRF和BIC等多种听觉电生理范式中验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有听觉生理计算模型多针对特定响应或通路阶段，难以跨实验范式与神经时间尺度整合发现。

Method: 采集92名被试约250小时EEG数据，涵盖不同电极蒙太奇及短音、言语、音乐刺激；训练因果神经网络，将双耳声波形直接映射到高采样率EEG；随后检验模型对ABR刺激速率/频率/呈现方式效应、连续言语的皮层下与皮层TRF、以及click诱发BIC的还原能力。

Result: 模型预测的ABR与TRF重现了既有响应形态及刺激依赖效应，模型总平均相关落在被试级人类分布范围内；预测的BIC指标与文献报道值高度接近。

Conclusion: 单一音频到EEG模型可跨范式与时间尺度捕捉听觉生理特征，支持未来in silico实验与听力技术应用。

Abstract: Computational models of auditory physiology commonly target specific responses or stages of the auditory pathway, limiting their ability to integrate findings across experimental paradigms and neural timescales. We present a foundation model of human auditory electrophysiology: a causal neural network trained to map binaural acoustic waveforms directly to high-sample-rate EEG. The model was trained on approximately 250 hours of EEG data from 92 subjects, with varied electrode montages and stimuli spanning tonebursts, speech, and music. We tested whether the model recovered effects of stimulus rate, frequency, and presentation method on auditory brainstem responses (ABRs); subcortical and cortical temporal response functions (TRFs) to continuous speech; and the click-evoked binaural interaction component (BIC). Predicted ABRs and TRFs reproduced established response morphology and stimulus-dependent effects, with model-grand-average correlations falling within the corresponding subject-level human distributions. The model-predicted BIC metrics closely resembled the values reported in the literature. These findings demonstrate that a single audio-to-EEG model can capture auditory physiology across paradigms and timescales, supporting future in silico experimentation and hearing technology applications.

</details>


<div id='physics.flu-dyn'></div>

# physics.flu-dyn [[Back]](#toc)

### [111] [Perturbation Theory for Translating Oblate-Spheroidal Droplets with Internal Circulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19193)
*William A. Sirignano*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 该文针对低We数（We<1）、Re=O(10)条件下轴对称液滴在气体中平移的形变问题，建立了耦合界面边界层、表面张力压差与势流理论的分析框架，预测了近椭球液滴形状、内部准稳态流场（含涡环结构）及气相绕流势场，并给出了液滴阻力的相关讨论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 液滴在气体中运动时，表面气压分布不均导致其偏离球形；理解低We数、中等Re数下液滴的形变与内外流场耦合机制，对于气液两相流动的阻力预测与流场建模具有重要意义。

Method: 基于局部压力跃变与两个曲率半径的关系分析形变；考虑气液界面两侧薄边界层，包含由压力梯度驱动、大密度比引起的表面速度跃变以及表面张力导致的压力跃变；以We为扰动参数预测近椭球形状；求解准稳态液相内部流函数与速度场；用位于液滴内部的环形偶极子作为镜像描述气相绕流，并建立环形偶极子半径与We的关系；同时采用精确解析解和基于√We的扰动分析进行对比，后者计算成本更低；将曲率、液体环流与气体势流三种分析匹配，以获得速度场与压力场。

Result: 预测了近椭球液滴形状、描述内部环流及涡环结构的准稳态液相流函数与速度场，给出了液滴外气相势流结果；发现镜像环形偶极子半径与√We相匹配，并获得了两种流体中的流函数、两个速度分量以及气相势场。

Conclusion: 通过匹配局部曲率、液体环流与气体势流三种分析，可一致地求出液滴内外速度场与压力场；基于√We的扰动分析在保持精度的同时降低了计算成本，并给出了关于液滴阻力的若干讨论。

Abstract: Liquid droplets deform from spherical shape due to aerodynamic variation of pressure along the surface as the droplet moves through a gas. The deformation is predicted for axisymmetric droplets translating through a gas with low Weber numbers, We < 1, and Reynolds number Re = O(10). That deformation analysis is based on the relations between local pressure jump and the two radii of curvature. A thin boundary layer on both sides of the gas-liquid interface is considered with a surface-velocity jump due to pressure-gradient-driven flow with a large density jump and a pressure jump due to surface tension. A near-ellipsoidal shape is predicted using $We$ as a perturbation parameter. Then, the quasi-steady internal liquid-phase stream function and velocity field are predicted, describing internal circulation and a vortex ring structure with vorticity distributed through an inviscid liquid. The gas-phase flow over the oblate droplet is described using a ring doublet as an image within the droplet. The ring-doublet radius is related to We. Gas potential flow results are presented and compared using both the exact analytical solution and a perturbation analysis based on the square root of We. The perturbation analysis provides a lower computational cost. Three analyses for local curvature, liquid circulation, and gas potential flow are matched to yield the velocity and pressure fields. The appropriate radius for the image ring doublet is matched to the square root of We. Stream functions and the two velocity components in both fluids and the gas potential field are predicted. Some comments on droplet drag are presented.

</details>


### [112] [The Level Set Ensemble Kalman Filter: Sequential Data Assimilation for Flows With Shocks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19314)
*Michael K. Sleeman,Lorenzo Beronilla,Hangchuan Hu,Xu-Hui Zhou,Matthias Morzfeld,Andrew M. Stuart,Tamer A. Zaki*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文提出 level set EnKF，通过在由水平集函数与光滑状态扩展构成的潜空间中进行分析步骤，解决了标准 EnKF 在含激波可压缩流中产生伪振荡的问题，并在多个一维可压缩流与二维爆炸波算例上验证了可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 集合卡尔曼滤波（EnKF）因鲁棒性与高维可扩展性被广泛用于科学与工程中的序贯数据同化。但标准 EnKF 应用于含激波的可压缩流时会产生伪振荡，其根源在于集合成员间激波位置的不确定性——激波位置的集合离散导致分析步中不同成员的状态被错误地线性组合。

Method: 提出 level set EnKF：构造从原始状态空间到潜空间的非线性映射，并在潜空间中执行分析步。潜表示包含两部分：(1) 编码不连续面位置的水平集函数；(2) 定义于整个物理域上的光滑状态扩展，用以表示不连续面两侧的解。作者首先将 level set EnKF 形式化为“潜空间数据同化”这一更一般框架的一个具体实例。

Result: 在若干一维可压缩流算例以及一个二维爆炸波算例上验证了 level set EnKF 的可行性。

Conclusion: 将分析步移至水平集潜空间可有效规避因激波位置集合不确定性引起的伪振荡，且该方法可纳入潜空间数据同化的通用框架，具备推广到更广泛不连续流场同化问题的潜力。

Abstract: The ensemble Kalman filter is commonly used to perform sequential data assimilation in science and engineering because of its robust performance and scalability when the state space dimension is high. However, the standard EnKF generates spurious oscillations when applied to compressible flows with shocks; the cause is the uncertainty, across the ensemble, in the shock location. This paper develops the \emph{level set EnKF}, which overcomes this challenge by employing a nonlinear mapping from the original state space into a latent space in which the analysis step is undertaken. The latent representation comprises a level set function that encodes the discontinuity location and smooth state extensions, defined over the full physical domain, that represent the solution on either side of the discontinuity. We first formulate the level set EnKF as a specific instance of a more general approach of performing data assimilation in a latent space. We then demonstrate the feasibility of the level set EnKF for several one-dimensional compressible flows and a two-dimensional blast wave.

</details>


### [113] [Fredholm Backstepping with Heymann Pre-Feedback for Linearized Navier--Stokes 2D Channel](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19331)
*Miroslav Krstic*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文对二维线性化Poiseuille槽道流实现任意雷诺数下的镇定，并可将各波数衰减率指定为任意值；提出首个针对Navier–Stokes系统的Fredholm反步设计，输入仅用单侧壁面两个速度分量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VK'07的Volterra反步设计依赖壁面法向输入使系统空间因果、再由切向输入闭合回路，且Fredholm反步通常需假设Orr–Sommerfeld算子特征值单重并排除若干系数取值。本文旨在将Fredholm方法推广至迄今最苛刻的被控对象（线性化Poiseuille流），去除这些限制。

Method: 仅使用单侧壁面两个速度分量作动。法向输入施加任意小增益的有限秩反馈，其作用（按Heymann引理意义）是使系统具有单重谱并让每个模态从切向速度可控；切向输入再施加镇定反馈，该反馈通过可逆Fredholm变换将系统映射到被任意衰减率左移的Stokes目标PDE得到。不假设Orr–Sommerfeld算子特征值单重，并移除了Fredholm反步中一贯的系数取值排除条件。

Result: 实现了线性化二维Poiseuille槽道流在任意雷诺数下的镇定，且每个波数的衰减率可任意指定；在雷诺数30000下完成反馈测试，闭环谱主导部分配置为使流动减速约二十倍。

Conclusion: 本文展示了Fredholm方法在其迄今最苛刻被控对象上的能力。与VK'07对比：Fredholm架构更强大，但其增益是谱形式的，而VK'07为闭式、更简单，并且Fredholm方法正是从VK'07借鉴了模型、作动方式与目标。

Abstract: We stabilize the linearized 2-D Poiseuille channel flow at arbitrary Reynolds numbers, and assign the decay of every wavenumber to any user-prescribed rate. The actuation is as in Vazquez--Krstic (IEEE TAC, 2007), VK'07 below: the two velocity components at only one wall. This is the first Fredholm backstepping design for a Navier--Stokes system. In VK'07 the wall-normal input renders the plant spatially causal, after which the tangential input closes a Volterra backstepping loop. Here the normal input carries a finite-rank feedback of arbitrarily small gain, whose sole role, in the sense of Heymann's lemma (TAC, 1968), is to give the plant simple spectrum and make every mode controllable from the tangential velocity. The tangential input then applies a stabilizing feedback, obtained through an invertible Fredholm transform to a Stokes target PDE, shifted left by an arbitrary decay. No simplicity of eigenvalues of the Orr--Sommerfeld operator is assumed and the exclusions of plant coefficient values that have accompanied Fredholm backstepping are removed. We state the comparison with the Volterra design without embellishment: the Fredholm architecture is powerful, but its gain is spectral where VK'07 is closed-form, the simpler of the two, and one from which the Fredholm approach takes its model, its actuation, and its target. What is demonstrated here is the reach of the Fredholm method on its most demanding plant to date. The feedback is tested at Reynolds number $30000$, with the dominant part of the closed-loop spectrum assigned so that it amounts to slowing the flow about twentyfold.

</details>


### [114] [How universal are warm turbulent cascades?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19432)
*Ben Y. Israeli,Gregory Falkovich*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文通过时间尺度论证与数值模拟证明：热阻尼对波湍流级联的非局域效应会使稳态解额外依赖紫外截断，从而质疑"暖级联"仅由守恒通量和温度参数化的普适性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 波动动力学方程的一般稳态解通常被认为仅由守恒通量和温度描述，这类由这些量参数化的解族被称为"暖级联"，并可由依赖局域相互作用假设的微分模型导出。作者希望检验这一局域性假设在热阻尼存在时是否成立。

Method: 采用时间尺度论证（time scale argument），分析热阻尼对级联的非局域效应，分别针对三波和四波相互作用系统进行考察；随后针对阻尼导致的级联耗竭进行数值验证。

Result: 热阻尼对级联的非局域效应使稳态解额外依赖于紫外截断这一参数，表明暖级联解的有效性受到质疑；数值实验展示了该行为的非局域性与非普适性（nonuniversal）。

Conclusion: 暖级联解并非普遍有效，其依赖于局域相互作用假设；在热阻尼作用下，稳态解需引入紫外截断等额外参数，级联被阻尼耗竭，呈现非局域、非普适特征。

Abstract: It is often assumed that general stationary solutions to the wave kinetic equation are described solely by conserved fluxes and temperature. The families of solutions parameterized by these quantities are referred to as warm cascades, and are derivable from differential models of wave kinetics. These models rely on the assumption of local interactions, and we demonstrate by a time scale argument that the nonlocal effect of thermal damping on the cascade may result in dependence on an additional parameter, the UV cutoff, calling into question the validity of warm cascade solutions. This is shown for systems with both three-wave and four-wave interactions. We then consider the depletion of the cascade by this damping, and present a numerical demonstration of this nonlocal and nonuniversal behavior.

</details>


### [115] [Wake interactions drive synchronized vortex merging in a hovering quadcopter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19507)
*Elias S. Pratschke,Claus C. Wolf,Daniel Schanz,Andreas Schröder,Oliver T. Schmidt*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文通过拉格朗日粒子追踪获得的悬停四旋翼三维速度场数据，发现并表征了一种跨旋翼涡同步合并的全局相干结构：四个旋翼涡在合并事件中频率同步，在转子频带以下形成非公倍频率的谱峰，且无法用单旋翼气动、次谐波不稳定或三波相互作用解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 悬停全尺寸四旋翼中最具能量的相干结构尚未被清楚认识。单个旋翼的尾迹特征（如叶尖涡及其次谐波配对）已有成熟认识，但多旋翼相互作用是否产生超越单旋翼气动的全局相干模式尚不明确。作者希望识别并刻画这种跨旋翼的全局相干结构，并检验其是否可由已有机制（单旋翼气动、次谐波不稳定、频带间三波相互作用）解释。此外，该类数据集存在高雷诺数湍流、主动飞控导致转速时变、低频机体漂移、有限时空分辨率与测量不确定性等复杂因素，需验证现象在复杂实验条件下的稳健性。

Method: 数据来自Shake-The-Box拉格朗日粒子追踪并经过同化的三维速度场。分析方法包括谱模态分析与条件分析（条件平均）。作者将观测到的谱特征与若干候选机制进行对比排除：单旋翼气动、次谐波不稳定性、频带间三波（triadic）相互作用。同时结合频域与时间域两类互补分析，在转速变化等实验复杂条件下检验该全局同步涡合并现象的稳健性。

Result: 多数相干结构呈现已知的单旋翼特征，如叶尖涡及其次谐波配对。全局同步涡合并表现为位于旋翼频带以下、非公倍（incommensurate）频率处的谱峰，无法用单旋翼气动、次谐波不稳定或频带间三波相互作用解释。条件平均给出证据表明该现象是间歇性的、明显非次谐波的涡合并过程，且涉及全部四个旋翼尾迹。作者指出，该现象是否在不同飞行条件与构型下普遍存在仍属推测；但其在实验复杂性（尤其是转速变化）下仍能被频域与时间域互补分析一致刻画，说明该现象具有稳健性。

Conclusion: 悬停全尺寸四旋翼存在一种由四个旋翼涡频率同步并伴随合并事件而形成的自组织、全局相关结构，其标志是转子频带以下的非公倍频率谱峰。该现象不能归因于单旋翼气动、次谐波不稳定或三波相互作用，而是一种间歇、非次谐波的跨旋翼涡合并。其在转速时变等复杂实验条件下仍可被互补的频域与时域方法一致识别，体现了稳健性；但跨飞行条件与构型的普适性仍需进一步验证。

Abstract: The most energetic coherent structure of a hovering full-scale quadcopter is associated with a self-organizing process in which the individual rotor vortices synchronize their frequencies while undergoing merging events, yielding a globally correlated structure. We identify and characterize this phenomenon by applying spectral modal and conditional analyses to assimilated three-dimensional velocity data acquired via Shake-The-Box Lagrangian particle tracking. The dataset captures a high-Re, turbulent flow further complicated by time-varying rotor speeds stemming from active flight control, low-frequency vehicle drift, finite spatio-temporal resolution, and measurement uncertainty. Most coherent structures recover established single-rotor features such as tip vortices and their subharmonic pairing. The globally synchronized vortex merging manifests as a spectral peak at an incommensurate frequency below the rotor band, which cannot be explained by single-rotor aerodynamics, subharmonic instabilities, or band-to-band triadic interactions. Instead, conditional averaging provides evidence of the aforementioned intermittent, distinctly non-subharmonic vortex-merging process involving all four rotor wakes. Establishing whether or not this phenomenon is observed across different flight conditions and configurations remains speculative; however, the consistent characterization of the globally synchronized vortex merging using complementary frequency- and time-domain analyses despite experimental complexities, in particular rotor speed variations, demonstrates its robustness.

</details>


### [116] [Well-posedness of neural turbulence closures and tangent dissipation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19647)
*Zhen Zhang,George Em Karniadakis*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 该论文研究神经湍流闭合所定义的新边值问题，提出通过“切向耗散”来保证解的存在性、唯一性和逆敏感性界，并在槽道流中验证两类受限闭合的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经湍流闭合会改变原有平均流算子，形成耦合的边值问题，其适定性（存在性、唯一性、稳定性）尚缺乏理论保证；同时非负涡黏性并不能控制切向扩散的矫顽性，因此需要研究如何促进切向耗散。

Method: 建立耦合边值问题 R(U)=N(U)+F(U)=0 及耦合雅可比 J=N'(U)+F'(U)；基于全局切向耗散给出两个结论；对单调原算子，由原算子与闭合共同提供正的一致余量以证明存在性、唯一性和全局逆敏感性界；对一般原算子分析耗散闭合对切向耗散的影响；提出两种促进切向耗散的方式：精确积分构造（强制切向扩散非负、放缩反应项）与在采样状态上对切向反应违规施加惩罚。

Result: 在 Re_τ=180–5200 的槽道流中，两类受限闭合在全部 50 组训练种子/雷诺数情形下均得到精确解；Re_τ=1000 时对每个闭合的固定网络进行 10,000 次初值测试，受限闭合各找到一个根，其他闭合出现多个根；Re_τ=5200 时，精确积分构造与惩罚分别将报告逆敏感性相对原算子降低约 372 倍与 11 倍。

Conclusion: 全局切向耗散为神经湍流闭合提供了适定性的理论工具；两类促进切向耗散的受限闭合在实践中能获得精确解并显著改善逆敏感性，10,000 次初值测试为受限闭合的唯一性提供了强经验证据，但并非严格证明。

Abstract: A neural turbulence closure defines a new boundary-value problem, $R(U)=N(U)+F(U)=0$, with a coupled Jacobian $J(U)=N'(U)+F'(U)$, where $N$ is the original mean-flow operator and $F$ the learned closure. We establish two consequences of global tangent dissipation. For a monotone original operator, a positive uniform margin supplied by the original operator and closure together guarantees existence, uniqueness and a global inverse-sensitivity bound relating a posteriori solution error to the a priori residual. For a general original operator, a dissipative closure cannot worsen tangent dissipation, but this alone does not guarantee uniqueness. Tangent dissipation depends on both diffusion and reaction. We study two complementary ways to promote it: (1) an exact-integral construction enforcing non-negative tangent diffusion while leaving reaction unconstrained, and (2) a penalty on tangent-reaction violations at sampled states. Tangent diffusion enters the Jacobian, and non-negative secant eddy viscosity alone does not control its coercivity. We conduct tests with channel flow at $Re_τ=180$--$5200$, which provides a strongly monotone baseline. Both constrained closures reach accurate solutions in all 50 training-seed/Reynolds-number cases. At $Re_τ=1000$, we conduct tests with 10,000 starts for one fixed network per closure and we find one root for each constrained closure and multiple roots for the other closures. Although this does not prove uniqueness, it provides strong empirical evidence for uniqueness of the tested constrained closures. At $Re_τ=5200$, the construction and penalty reduce the reported inverse sensitivity relative to the original operator by approximately $372\times$ and $11\times$, respectively.

</details>


### [117] [Experimental study of the impact dynamics of polymeric hollow droplets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19667)
*Mohammad Mahdi Nasiri,Mohammad Reza Daneshvar Garmroodi,Damian Vadillo,Moussa Tembely*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本研究实验考察了空心牛顿（水）与黏弹性（聚合物溶液）液滴撞击固体表面的动力学，揭示了中心反喷流形成、气泡破裂延迟、灯串状结构及沉积/部分沉积/脱离三种结局，阐明惯性、黏性、毛细与弹性共同支配空心中非牛顿液滴的铺展形态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空心液滴撞击在涂覆、喷涂等应用中具有重要意义，但其动力学研究远少于实心液滴，尤其黏弹性空心液滴的撞击动力学尚未被充分探索。

Method: 实验研究空心牛顿液滴（水）与黏弹性液滴（聚合物溶液）在不同撞击速度和聚合物浓度下撞击固体表面的行为，系统改变聚合物浓度与撞击速度。

Result: 观察到空心液滴铺展的两大特征：中心反喷流形成与最终沉积，均与内裹气泡相关。牛顿液滴的反喷流因毛细不稳定性快速生长并破碎；加入聚合物后，黏弹性改变反喷流高度与速度，延迟气泡破裂并抑制液滴脱离；丝状细化过程中出现灯串（beads-on-a-string）结构，体现弹性与毛细力的竞争。识别出沉积、部分沉积、脱离三种结局的条件。

Conclusion: 惯性、黏性、毛细与弹性共同支配空心非牛顿液滴的溅射/铺展形态，黏弹性显著改变反喷流动力学与气泡破裂行为。

Abstract: The impact dynamics of hollow droplets, while influential in applications such as coating and spraying, remain less explored than their dense counterparts. In particular, the impact dynamics of viscoelastic hollow droplets have yet to be fully explored. This study presents an experimental investigation into the impact of hollow Newtonian (water) and viscoelastic (polymeric solution) droplets on a solid surface at different impact velocities and polymer concentrations. We demonstrate two hallmark features of hollow droplet flattening: the formation of a central counter-jet and the final deposition, both associated with the entrapped air bubble. For Newtonian droplets, the counter-jet exhibits rapid growth and breakup due to capillary instabilities. Introducing polymer additives fundamentally alters this behavior: viscoelasticity affects the counter-jet's height and velocity, delays bubble rupture, and inhibits droplet detachment. Crucially, we observe the emergence of beads-on-a-string structures during filament thinning, a signature of the competition between elastic and capillary forces. By systematically varying the polymer concentration and impact velocity, we identify the conditions under which three distinct outcomes occur: deposition, partial deposition, and detachment. Our results show how inertia, viscosity, capillarity, and elasticity together govern the splashing morphology of hollow non-Newtonian droplets.

</details>


### [118] [Mixed-precision GPU algorithms for efficient turbulent flow simulations with Raviart-Thomas finite elements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19828)
*Ivan Prusak,Enes Mustafa Soydan,Ivan Pribec,Martin Kronbichler*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文提出用于不可压缩湍流高保真模拟的GPU算法，结合H(div)协调高阶Raviart-Thomas有限元、L2协调间断Galerkin压力近似、基于高阶BDF的相容分裂格式，以及全矩阵无运算与多重网格求解器，并提出鲁棒混合精度框架，通过最小二乘投影生成迭代求解器初值，实现最高1.7倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 不可压缩湍流高保真模拟计算量大，需在GPU上实现高效求解框架；同时希望在保证湍流统计量不变的前提下，通过混合精度计算降低计算成本、提升求解效率。

Method: 空间离散采用速度的H(div)协调高阶Raviart-Thomas有限元与压力的L2协调间断Galerkin近似；时间推进采用基于高阶BDF步进的相容分裂格式，对流项显式处理；每个时间步需解压力泊松方程与速度的对称反应扩散型方程。开发了所有组件的快速矩阵无算子求值框架，对泊松问题采用多重网格求解器，并提出鲁棒混合精度算法框架，关键是用最小二乘投影为迭代线性求解器生成高精度初值。

Result: 利用最小二乘投影生成初值后，可将迭代线性求解器的相对残差容差设为10^-3。在该容差下，完全单精度运行对整体迭代次数几乎无影响，并保持关键湍流统计量，相比纯双精度模拟获得最高1.7倍加速。

Conclusion: 所提GPU混合精度求解框架可在不牺牲湍流统计量精度的情况下有效加速不可压缩湍流的高保真模拟，验证了最小二乘投影初值与单精度求解相结合的可行性与效率优势。

Abstract: We propose GPU algorithms for high-fidelity simulation of incompressible turbulent flows. Discretization in space is performed with H(div)-conforming high-order Raviart-Thomas finite elements for the velocity and an $L^2$-conforming discontinuous Galerkin approximation for the pressure. In time, a consistent splitting scheme based on higher-order BDF time stepping is used, with convection treated explicitly. In this scheme, a pressure Poisson equation and a symmetric reaction-diffusion-type equation for the velocity need to be solved in each time step. We develop a solution framework with fast matrix-free operator evaluation for all ingredients, combined with multigrid solvers for the Poisson problem, and propose a robust mixed-precision algorithmic framework. A key to mixed-precision efficiency is a least-squares projection to generate accurate initial guesses for the iterative linear solvers, enabling us to work with relative residual tolerances of $10^{-3}$. In this regime, running the solvers entirely in single precision leads to almost no change in overall iteration counts and maintains the crucial turbulence statistics, while showing up to $1.7\times$ speedup over pure double-precision simulations.

</details>


### [119] [Mean flow scaling in stably stratified temporally developing turbulent boundary layers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19935)
*Hardy Baptiste,Costa Pedro*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文利用时间发展湍流边界层（TTBL）框架开展直接数值模拟，系统研究从弱稳定到极稳定分层条件下的稳定分层壁湍流，验证并拓展了Monin–Obukhov相似理论（MOST），并提出了平均速度剖面截距的经验闭合关系及对MOST贡献的阻尼修正，从而在整个研究区间内准确预测壁面摩擦系数 $C_f$。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稳定分层壁湍流广泛存在于环境与工程流动中，其关键在于刻画分层如何改变平均与湍流剖面。MOST虽是主流建模框架，但很少在宽分层范围内用良好受控的DNS数据进行严格验证。因此作者希望借助TTBL框架，在广泛Reynolds与Richardson数范围内隔离浮力效应，系统检验MOST的适用性。

Method: 采用时间发展湍流边界层（TTBL）框架，对弱稳定至极稳定区间的分层湍流边界层进行直接数值模拟，覆盖较宽的Reynolds数与Richardson数范围，并将浮力效应与旋转等其他机制分离。基于模拟数据检验MOST，分析平均速度梯度、湍流切应力与热通量的衰减行为，并据此提出经验闭合与阻尼修正。

Result: TTBL设置能忠实再现经典相似理论结果；基于地表的平均速度梯度标度律在比以前报道更宽的 $z/L$ 范围内成立，这归因于该典型流动中湍流切应力与热通量具有相似的衰减率。随着分层增强，平均速度剖面的截距增大，直至维持对数区所需的尺度分离无法继续；作者提出了该截距偏移随基于Obukhov长度的Reynolds数的经验闭合。最后，对MOST在平均速度剖面中的贡献进行简单阻尼修正并通过验证，可准确预测研究区间内的壁面摩擦系数 $C_f$。

Conclusion: TTBL框架可有效研究稳定分层壁湍流，并支持MOST的扩展应用：基于地表的标度律适用范围更广，平均速度剖面截距随分层增强而增大并可经验闭合，经阻尼修正后能够准确预测壁面摩擦系数 $C_f$。

Abstract: Stably stratified wall-bounded turbulence governs the dynamics of many environmental and engineering flows. A key challenge is characterizing how stratification modifies mean and turbulent profiles. Monin--Obukhov similarity theory (MOST) is the dominant modelling framework, although it has rarely been rigorously validated against well-controlled direct numerical simulation (DNS) data over a wide range of stratification levels. In this study, we exploit the temporally developing turbulent boundary layer (TTBL) framework to investigate stratified turbulent boundary layers from the weakly stable to the very stable regime, spanning a range of Reynolds and Richardson numbers, and isolating the effects due to buoyancy from other mechanisms such as flow rotation. We demonstrate that the TTBL set-up faithfully reproduces classical similarity theory results and that surface-based scaling of the mean velocity gradient holds over a wider range of $z/L$ ($L$ being the Obukhov length) than previously reported. This result is attributed to the similar decay rate of turbulent shear stress and heat flux in this canonical flow. Next, we show that, as stratification intensifies, the intercept of the mean velocity profile increases, until the separation of scales required for a logarithmic region to exist can no longer be sustained. We propose an empirical closure for this intercept shift in terms of the Reynolds number based on the Obukhov length. Finally, a simple damping of the MOST contribution to the mean velocity profile is proposed and validated, enabling accurate prediction of the wall friction coefficient ($C_f$) across the investigated regimes.

</details>


### [120] [Universal mapping of drop impact spreading from wetting to Leidenfrost regimes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20259)
*Shushan Hu,Nan Hu,Zirui Li,Yifei Sun,Xiang Gao,Liwu Fan*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 该论文从能量耗散角度建立了液滴在润湿碰撞与Leidenfrost碰撞（存在稳定蒸气膜）条件下最大铺展之间的普适映射，实验与直接数值模拟验证其在Ohnesorge数和Weber数2–3个数量级范围内成立，为将润湿碰撞的既有预测结果直接转化为Leidenfrost情形提供了简便途径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Leidenfrost碰撞中液滴与基底间存在稳定蒸气膜，其动力学与润湿碰撞显著不同，通常需要分别建模；缺乏统一框架导致润湿碰撞的成熟预测难以直接用于Leidenfrost情形，预测与控制液滴碰撞行为受限。

Method: 从能量耗散角度出发，构建润湿碰撞与Leidenfrost碰撞最大铺展之间的普适映射；结合实验观测与直接数值模拟，并在Ohnesorge数与Weber数跨越2–3个数量级的参数空间内进行验证。

Result: 实验与直接数值模拟均表明，所建立的映射在Ohnesorge数和Weber数2–3个数量级范围内保持有效，能够可靠地将润湿碰撞的最大铺展预测转化为对应的Leidenfrost结果。

Conclusion: 该统一映射框架简化了Leidenfrost碰撞最大铺展的预测，为液滴碰撞的预测与调控提供了便捷工具，并对相关应用具有启示意义。

Abstract: We establish a universal mapping between the maximum spreading of a drop under wetting and Leidenfrost impact conditions from an energy-dissipation perspective, with the latter featuring a stable vapor film between the drop and the substrate. Experiments and direct numerical simulations demonstrate that the mapping remains valid over two to three orders of magnitude in the Ohnesorge and Weber numbers. This framework provides a simple route for converting established predictions for wetting impacts into their Leidenfrost counterparts, with implications for predicting and controlling droplet impact.

</details>


### [121] [Three-dimensional shock-weak-discontinuity interactions: curvature deposition and post-shock flow response](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20289)
*Alexander Omelchenko*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文推导了三维激波面上弱波（声波、熵波、涡波）扫过时所满足的局部欧拉连接律，将法向面散射问题与三维几何实现解耦：散射确定一个标量曲率幅值，激波取向与既有形状决定完整曲率张量及下游流动响应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 弱声波、熵波或涡片可以穿过有限强度三维激波而不产生折角，但会在相交曲线上沉积曲率间断。现有理论缺乏描述这一曲率通道的局部连接条件，无法为连续可微、分段二次可微激波阵面的光滑重构提供界面数据。

Method: 建立局部欧拉连接律，将主动的法向面散射问题与三维几何实现分离；用受控的鞍形激波相互作用对进行数值验证（入射与出射模态幅值相同）；对完全气体分析曲率中性分支；对传播熵波与面内涡片考察曲率沉积符号及其系数关系；以膨胀球激波作为非定常算例；在脐点附近分析主曲率框架的选择与旋转。

Result: 一对受控鞍形激波相互作用具有相同的入射与出射模态幅值，但激波后压力梯度矢量相差31.6度、涡量矢量相差25.6度。完全气体中声波入射存在曲率中性分支：出射声波、熵波与剪切波保持有限而曲率通道相消。传播熵波与面内涡片沉积的曲率符号相反，其系数满足由总焓守恒与声波正交性给出的精确关系；沿切线的涡量对曲率透明。膨胀球激波给出激波速度和交线跟踪速度均非零的非定常算例，可分离光滑爆炸波加速度与沉积加速度跳变。脐点附近该连接律可选择或旋转主曲率框架。

Conclusion: 所得到的连接律为跨连续可微、分段二次可微激波阵面连接光滑曲激波重构提供了所需的界面数据，揭示了弱波—激波相互作用中曲率沉积的普适定则与守恒约束。

Abstract: A weak acoustic, entropy or vortical sheet can cross a finite-strength three-dimensional shock without producing a kink, yet it can deposit a discontinuity of curvature along the intersection curve. We derive a local Euler junction law that separates the active normal-plane scattering problem from its three-dimensional geometric realization. The scattering selects one scalar curvature amplitude, while the orientation and pre-existing shape of the shock determine the full curvature tensor and the downstream flow response. A controlled pair of saddle-shock interactions has identical incident and outgoing modal amplitudes but produces post-shock pressure-gradient vectors separated by 31.6 degrees and vorticity vectors separated by 25.6 degrees. For a perfect gas, acoustic incidence also possesses curvature-neutral branches: outgoing acoustic, entropy and shear waves remain finite while the curvature channel cancels. Convected entropy and in-plane vortical sheets deposit curvature with opposite signs, and their coefficients obey an exact relation imposed by total-enthalpy conservation and acoustic orthogonality; line-tangent vorticity is curvature-transparent. An expanding spherical shock provides an unsteady example with non-zero shock speed and non-zero intersection-line tracking speed, separating the smooth blast-wave acceleration from the deposited acceleration jump. Near an umbilic, the same junction selects or rotates the principal-curvature frame. The resulting law supplies the interface data needed to connect smooth curved-shock reconstructions across a continuously differentiable, piecewise twice-differentiable front.

</details>


### [122] [Solutions of the Navier-Stokes Equation Through Affine Transformations: The Triad Triplet](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20710)
*Ö. D. Gürcan,L. Manfredini,P. Morel*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文在三维Navier-Stokes湍流螺旋分解的框架下，提出“三重 triad 组（triad triplet）”概念：即同一形状与类别的三个连续 triad，其参考波数分别对应最小、中间与最大波数。作者推导了三重 triad 组的演化方程，并给出与常能量/螺旋度通量对应的幂律精确解及一个孤立 triad 组上的时间依赖精确解（可由Jacobi椭圆函数的推广表达），这构成无粘极限下新的非线性精确解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 螺旋分解中单个 triad 只能描述局部相互作用，难以追踪给定形状和类别的 triad 在波数空间中的“局部能量转移”沿尺度方向的连续传递过程。作者试图构造一个能刻画这种跨尺度传递的最小对象，并寻找其精确解，以加深对湍流波数空间能量/螺旋度级串机制的理解。

Method: 将三个形状与类别相同、参考波数分别为最小/中间/最大的连续 triad 组合为“triad triplet”；对单个 triad 三条边的方程施加包含缩放、旋转和反射的仿射变换并加以耦合，从而得到 triad triplet 的演化方程；进而求解该方程组，寻找幂律解与时间依赖解，并与Jacobi椭圆函数进行类比推广。

Result: 得到 triad triplet 的演化方程；发现对应能量与螺旋度常通量的已知幂律解可作为 triad triplet 链的精确解，并对 triadic 相位给出具体含义；此外在孤立 triad triplet 上获得一个精确的时间依赖解，其形式可视为Jacobi椭圆函数的直接推广，该解是 Navier-Stokes 方程无粘极限的新的精确非线性解。

Conclusion: triad triplet 为追踪波数空间中由特定形状和类别 triad 引起的局部转移提供了有效工具；其链式结构支持常通量幂律解，孤立情形则给出可表为Jacobi椭圆函数推广的精确时间依赖解，从而丰富了无粘 Navier-Stokes 方程的精确非线性解集合。

Abstract: Considering triads in helical decomposition of three dimensional Navier-Stokes turbulence, three consecutive triads of the same shape and class, where the reference wave-number appears as the smallest, the middle and the largest wave-numbers respectively, constitutes an interesting object called the triad triplet that allows tracing the local transfer in wave-number space due to a given shape and class of triads. It is shown that, evolution equations for the triad triplet can be obtained from the equations of the three legs of a single triad that are put together via affine transformations involving scaling, rotation and reflection. Known power law solutions corresponding to constant flux of energy and helicity appear as exact solutions of a chain of triad triplets, with particular implications for the triadic phases. More interestingly, an exact, time dependent solution is available on an isolated triad triplet, that can be expressed using what appears to be straightforward generalization of Jacobi elliptic functions. These solutions, constitute novel exact nonlinear solutions of the inviscid limit of the Navier-Stokes equations.

</details>
